{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01EajuVdy9ieSqLw8QB8iCBk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Disk space analysis:\n- Total data disk: 250GB, Used: 191GB, Available: 60GB\n- modelscope_cache:\n  - Qwen2.5-14B FP16: 29GB\n  - Qwen2.5-14B AWQ: 9.4GB\n  - Qwen2.5-32B FP16 (incomplete 10/17): 37GB\n  - Llama-3.1-8B FP16: 30GB (wait, Llama 8B is 30GB? That seems large for an 8B model. Maybe it includes other files)\n\n\n- HF cache: 47GB (includes AWQ models for Qwen 32B, 72B, Mistral, Llama 8B + datasets)\n\nSo the models are taking up roughly 152GB of the 191GB used. The Llama 8B size is puzzling me though—at 16GB for the weights alone in float16, that extra 14GB suggests there might be additional files or maybe the original uncompressed versions stored alongside. Let me dig into what's actually in that directory.\n\nLooking at what I need to prioritize: Qwen 14B is fully covered with both FP16 and AWQ versions, Llama 8B has the FP16 but I'm missing the AWQ quantization, and Qwen 32B is only partially downloaded in FP16 while the AWQ slot is empty. For the immediate next steps, grabbing the Llama 8B AWQ would be straightforward at around 5GB, but completing the Qwen 32B setup is trickier—I'd need another 28GB to finish the FP16 plus 9GB for AWQ, totaling 37GB more when I only have 60GB free.\n\nBut there's a bigger constraint I'm realizing: the RTX 6000 has 24GB of VRAM, and neither the 14B nor 32B models in FP16 would fit in that—they'd need around 28GB and 64GB respectively. },\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(\\\\\\\"/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/artifacts/results/plan001\\\\\\\")\\\\nCKPT_PATH = OUTPUT_DIR / \\\\\\\"fp16_baseline_v2_checkpoint.json\\\\\\\"\\\\nDATA_PATH = OUTPUT_DIR / \\\\\\\"fp16_eval_data_v2.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef set_seed(seed):\\\\n    random.seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\\\\n        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef find_local_path(model_id, alt_ids=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Find model in local cache (HF or ModelScope) without downloading.\\\\n    alt_ids: additional model IDs to search (e.g. ModelScope org differs from HF).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    import glob\\\\n\\\\n    candidates = [model_id] + (alt_ids or [])\\\\n    for mid in candidates:\\\\n        # HF cache\\\\n        hf_name = mid.replace(\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"--\\\\\\\")\\\\n        hf_snaps = [])) >= N_SAMPLES:\\\\n            log.info(f\\\\\\\"Using cached eval data: {len(data['mmlu'])} MMLU, {len(data['arc'])} ARC\\\\\\\")\\\\n            return data[\\\\\\\"mmlu\\\\\\\"][:N_SAMPLES], data[\\\\\\\"arc\\\\\\\"][:N_SAMPLES]\\\\n\\\\n    log.info(f\\\\\\\"Preparing eval data: {N_SAMPLES} samples per benchmark...\\\\\\\")\\\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\\\n\\\\n    mmlu = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"cais/mmlu\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"all\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\")\\\\n    arc = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"allenai/ai2_arc\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"ARC-Challenge\\\\\\\", letter of the correct answer.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n        f\\\\\\\"Question: {question}\\\\\\\\n{opts}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAnswer:\\\\\\\"\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef extract_answer(text):\\\\n    text = text.strip()\\\\n    m = re.match(r'^[(\\\\\\\\[]?([A-Da-d])[)\\\\\\\\].]?', text)\\\\n    if m:\\\\n        return m.group(1).upper()\\\\n    m = re.search(r'(?:answer|correct)\\\\\\\\s*(?:is|:)\\\\\\\\s*[(\\\\\\\\[]?([A-Da-d])', text, re.IGNORECASE)\\\\n    if m:\\\\n        return m.group(1).upper()\\\\n    m = re.search(r'\\\\\\\\b([A-D])\\\\\\\\b', text)\\\\n    if m:\\\\n        return m.group(1)\\\\n    return None\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef release_gpu():\\\\n    gc.collect()\\\\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\\\\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\\\n    time.sleep(3)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef evaluate_model(model, tokenizer, samples, dataset_name, model_name=\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"):\\\\n    correct = 0\\\\n    total = 0\\\\n    for i, s in enumerate(samples):\\\\n        prompt = format_mcq(s[\\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\"], s[\\\\\\\"choices\\\\\\\"], s.get(\\\\\\\"labels\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        messages = [{\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": prompt}]\\\\n        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\\\n        inputs = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\").to(model.device)\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            outputs = model.generate(\\\\n                **inputs,\\\\n                max_new_tokens=1,\\\\n                do_sample=False,\\\\n                pad_token_id=tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\\\n            )\\\\n        response = tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][inputs[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)\\\\n        pred = extract_answer(response)\\\\n        gold = chr(65 + s[\\\\\\\"answer\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        if pred == gold:\\\\n            correct += 1\\\\n        total += 1\\\\n        if i < 3:\\\\n            log.info(f\\\\\\\"  [{model_name}] {dataset_name} #{i}: resp={response!r} pred={pred} gold={gold} {'OK' if pred==gold else 'WRONG'}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\\\\n            log.info(f\\\\\\\"  [{model_name}] {dataset_name}: {i+1}/{len(samples)}, acc={correct/total:.3f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef run_model_eval(model_path, tokenizer, mmlu_samples, arc_samples, model_name=\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\", is_awq=False):\\\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM\\\\n    log.info(f\\\\\\\"Loading {'AWQ' if is_awq else 'FP16'} model from {model_path}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n    t0 = time.time()\\\\n\\\\n    load_kwargs = {\\\\\\\"device_map\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"trust_remote_code\\\\\\\": True}\\\\n    if not is_awq:\\\\n        load_kwargs[\\\\\\\"torch_dtype\\\\\\\"] = torch.float16\\\\n\\\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_path, **load_kwargs)\\\\n    model.eval()\\\\n    log.info(f\\\\\\\"Loaded in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f}GB\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    mmlu_acc = evaluate_model(model, tokenizer, mmlu_samples, \\\\\\\"MMLU\\\\\\\", model_name)\\\\n    arc_acc = evaluate_model(model, tokenizer, arc_samples, \\\\\\\"ARC\\\\\\\", model_name)\\\\n    log.info(f\\\\\\\"[{model_name}] results: MMLU={mmlu_acc:.4f}, ARC={arc_acc:.4f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    del model\\\\n    release_gpu()\\\\n    return mmlu_acc, arc_acc\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_checkpoint():\\\\n    if CKPT_PATH.exists():\\\\n        with open(CKPT_PATH) as f:\\\\n            return
